releases](https://github.com/google/error-prone/releases).*\n\n> ## Error Prone 2.36.0\n> \n> Changes:\n> \n> -   Add new matcher interfaces to `ErrorProneScanner` for AST nodes introduced after Java 11 (e5fd194fa21ef9a01e8d4c72489906247aad81c8)\n> -   Fix compatibility with latest JDK 24 EA builds ([google/error-prone@d67bc15](https://github.com/google/error-prone/commit/d67bc156b737d13ac693d73a403a11a97804423f))\n> -   Check that `--should-stop=ifError=FLOW` is set when using the `-Xplugin` integration (e71db1f369a9367f6f2db34c4fbd006b6d6238fd)\n> \n> New checks:\n> \n> -   [`DuplicateBranches`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/DuplicateBranches): Discourage conditional expressions and if statements where both branches are the same\n> -   [`RedundantControlFlow`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/RedundantControlFlow): Reports redundant `continue` statements.\n> \n> Closed issues: [#4633](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4633), [#4646](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4646)\n> \n> Full changelog: [google/error-prone@v2.35.1...v2.36.0](https://github.com/google/error-prone/compare/v2.35.1...v2.36.0)\n> \n> ## Error Prone 2.35.1\n> \n> Error Prone's dependency on protobuf has been downgraded to 3.25.5 for this release.\n> \n> Version 3.25.5 of protobuf still fixes [CVE-2024-7254](https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-735f-pc8j-v9w8 \"CVE-2024-7254\"). This release is provided for users who aren't ready to update to 4.x, see also [#4584](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4584) and [#4634](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4634). Future versions of Error Prone will upgrade back to protobuf 4.x.\n> \n> Full changelog: [google/error-prone@v2.35.0...v2.35.1](https://github.com/google/error-prone/compare/v2.35.0...v2.35.1)\n> \n> ## Error Prone 2.35.0\n> \n> Changes:\n> \n> -   Fix handling of `\\s` before the trailing delimiter in [`MisleadingEscapedSpace`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/MisleadingEscapedSpace)\n> -   [`TimeUnitMismatch`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/TimeUnitMismatch) improvements: handle binary trees, consider trees like `fooSeconds * 1000` to have units of millis\n> \n> New checks:\n> \n> -   [`JavaDurationGetSecondsToToSeconds`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/JavaDurationGetSecondsToToSeconds): Prefer `duration.toSeconds()` over `duration.getSeconds()`\n> \n> Full changelog: [google/error-prone@v2.34.0...v2.35.0](https://github.com/google/error-prone/compare/v2.34.0...v2.35.0)\n> \n> ## Error Prone 2.34.0\n> \n> Changes:\n> \n> -   Passing the javac flag `--should-stop=ifError=FLOW` is now required when running Error Prone ([#4595](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4595))\n> -   The `MemberName` check was renamed to [`IdentifierName`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/IdentifierName)\n> \n> New checks:\n> \n> -   [`FutureTransformAsync`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/FutureTransformAsync): The usage of transformAsync is not necessary when all the return values of the transformation function are immediate futures.\n> -   [`MisformattedTestData`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/MisformattedTestData): Reformats Java source code inside Error Prone test cases.\n> -   [`MisleadingEscapedSpace`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/MisleadingEscapedSpace): Using `\\s` anywhere except at the end of a line in a text block is potentially misleading.\n> -   [`ThrowIfUncheckedKnownUnchecked`](https://errorprone.info/bugpattern/ThrowIfUncheckedKnownUnchecked): `throwIfUnchecked(knownUnchecked)` is equivalent to `throw knownUnchecked`.\n> \n> Closed issues: [#4595](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4595), [#4598](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4598), [#4620](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4620)\n\n...   `SING_SINGLETON_HAS_NONPRIVATE_CONSTRUCTOR` is reported in case of a non-private constructor,\n>     -   `SING_SINGLETON_IMPLEMENTS_CLONEABLE` is reported in case of a class directly implementing the `Cloneable` interface,\n>     -   `SING_SINGLETON_INDIRECTLY_IMPLEMENTS_CLONEABLE` is reported when a class indirectly implements the `Cloneable` interface,\n>     -   `SING_SINGLETON_IMPLEMENTS_CLONE_METHOD` is reported when a class does not implement the `Cloneable` interface, but has a `clone()` method,\n>     -   `SING_SINGLETON_IMPLEMENTS_SERIALIZABLE` is reported when a class directly or indirectly implements the `Serializable` interface and\n>     -   `SING_SINGLETON_GETTER_NOT_SYNCHRONIZED` is reported when the instance-getter method of the singleton class is not synchronized. (See [SEI CERT MSC07-J](https://wiki.sei.cmu.edu/confluence/display/java/MSC07-J.+Prevent+multiple+instantiations+of+singleton+objects))\n> -   Extend `FindOverridableMethodCall` detector with new bug type: `MC_OVERRIDABLE_METHOD_CALL_IN_READ_OBJECT`. It's reported when an overridable method is called from `readObject()`, according to SEI CERT rule [SER09-J. Do not invoke overridable methods from the readObject() method](https://wiki.sei.cmu.edu/confluence/display/java/SER09-J.+Do+not+invoke+overridable+methods+from+the+readObject%28%29+method).\n\n... 3.25.5 for this release.\n> \n> Version 3.25.5 of protobuf still fixes [CVE-2024-7254](https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-735f-pc8j-v9w8 \"CVE-2024-7254\"). This release is provided for users who aren't ready to update to 4.x, see also [#4584](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4584) and [#4634](https://redirect.github.com/google/error-prone/issues/4634). Future versions of Error Prone will upgrade back to protobuf 4.x.\n> \n> Full changelog: [google/error-prone@v2.35.0...v2.35.1](https://github.com/google/error-prone/compare/v2.35.0...v2.35.1)\n> \n> ## Error Prone 2.35.0\n> \n> Changes:\n> \n> -
